### Riyadh Cement Co. Announces Contract Sign Off with SAMANA Energy‏

[3092\\
\\
RIYADH CEMENT\\
\\
 -4.91\\
% \\
\\
1448/04/03     14/09/2026 15:38:50](https://www.saudiexchange.sa/wps/portal/saudiexchange/hidden/company-profile-main/!ut/p/z1/04_Sj9CPykssy0xPLMnMz0vMAfIjo8ziTR3NDIw8LAz83d2MXA0C3SydAl1c3Q0NvE30I4EKzBEKDMKcTQzMDPxN3H19LAzdTU31w8syU8v1wwkpK8hOMgUA-oskdg!!/?companySymbol=3092#Z7_5A602H80O0VC4060O4GML81G55)

| Element List | Explanation |
| --- | --- |
| Introduction | In line with the objectives of Saudi Arabia’s Vision 2030 and its focus on sustainability and clean energy, Riyadh Cement Company announces the award and signing of a 25-year agreement with Samana Energy for the purchase of solar energy. |
| Date of Announcement of the Award | 2026-09-14 Corresponding to 1448-04-03 |
| Contract Subject Matter | Purchase of energy from SAMANA Energy |
| The Entity with Which the Contract was Signed | SAMANA Energy‏ |
| Date of Signing the Contract | 2026-09-14 Corresponding to 1448-04-03 |
| Contract Value | The average annual payments under the agreement are expected to be approximately SAR 6 million per year, excluding VAT, throughout the contract term. |
| Contract Details | The agreement is based on purchasing energy from SANAMA Energy at a cost lower than the Company’s current electricity generation cost and lower than the cost of Saudi Energy Company. Riyadh Cement Company will not be required to provide financing for the project, as it will not incur any capital or operating expenditures, subject to obtaining the necessary licenses and approvals from the relevant authorities. |
| Contract Duration | 25 years |
| Financial Impact and the Relevant Period | The power generation system is expected to commence operations in the fourth quarter of 2027. Based on the Company’s initial estimates, the impact of cost savings is expected to materialize gradually from the first year of operations, with the actual financial impact to be determined based on the energy prices applicable to the energy consumed from the project at that time. |
| Related Parties | N/A |

The Capital Market Authority and Saudi Exchange take no responsibility for the contents of this disclosure, make no representations as to its accuracy or completeness, and expressly disclaim any liability whatsoever for any loss arising from, or incurred in reliance upon, any part of this disclosure, and the issuer accepts full responsibility for the accuracy of the information contained in it and confirms, having made all reasonable enquiries, that to the best of their knowledge and belief, there are no other facts or information the omission of which would make the disclosure misleading, incomplete or inaccurate.
